# Audit: Known unknowns triage

Generated by `python3 -I tools/unknowns_triage.py . --out docs/audit/KNOWN_UNKNOWNS_TRIAGE.md --json tools/out/unknowns.json --override docs/audit/unknowns_overrides.json` (see [tools/README.md](../../tools/README.md#8-known-unknowns-triage)); do not edit by hand. Each row is one bullet of a `## Known unknowns` section. `line` is the line of the bullet in the page as it was when the tool ran. Text is cut at 160 characters and links are flattened.

| class | count | share |
|---|---|---|
| asm | 418 | 26.5% |
| server | 770 | 48.8% |
| device | 258 | 16.3% |
| unclear | 133 | 8.4% |

Classes: `asm` = resolvable by reading more of the disassembly; `server` = the answer is on the server side; `device` = needs a real device, a capture or firmware knowledge; `unclear` = no rule matched and the manual pass left it open. Rules are ordered keyword matches (server, then device, then asm; first match wins), see `RULES` in the tool.
